==============================================================================
TESSERA — SINGLE-PIECE SOLUTION CENSUS
3-D PYRAMID — 5 layers, 5x5 base
==============================================================================

One piece is placed on an otherwise empty target and the remaining eleven
are asked to finish it. Every orientation of every piece, at every position
it fits, is listed below — grouped by piece, most solutions first.

Complete fills of the empty target : 2,448
Positions examined                 : 1,646
Positions with no solution         : 761
Enumeration time                   : 6.7s
Generated                          : 2026-09-08T23:13:16.520Z

Each piece's numbers sum to the total above: a complete fill uses every
piece exactly once, so the fills are partitioned by where that piece went.
Reflections and rotations of the whole target are counted separately, the
same way the game's own solver counts them.

------------------------------------------------------------------------------
HOW TO READ A POSITION
------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Sockets are named L<layer><row letter><column number>. Layer 1 is the 5x5
base and layer 5 the single top bead; within a layer, rows are lettered from
A and columns numbered from 1. L1C4 is layer 1, row 3, column 4.

  F-o7@L1A1   L1A1 L1A2 L1B1   L1:##.../#..../...../...../.....
  |  |  |     |                |
  |  |  |     |                `- only the layers the piece touches, each drawn
  |  |  |     |                   as its rows separated by "/", "#" for the piece
  |  |  |     `- every socket the piece covers, lowest layer first
  |  |  `- the first of those sockets, which anchors the code
  |  `- which of the piece's 3-D orientations (a signed permutation of the
  |     lattice axes; the socket list above is the authoritative description)
  `- the piece

A code is unique: no two positions of the same piece cover the same sockets.
